# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Hedar (Mois de ẖedār)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 44  
**Columns**: pp. 238–406  

[p. 238] {#po.44.p238}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። ወርኃ ፡ ኅዳር ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ መዓልቱ ፡ ፲ሰዓት ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ የኃጽጽ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. The month of Hedär is blessed. Its first day is of ten hours; then the days decrease.

### READING OF THE FIRST HEDAR
አመ ፡ ፳ለኅዳር ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ መስተጋድላን ፡ ሰማዕታት፤ ፡ መክሲሞስ ፡ ወኑመንፍዮስ ፡ ወፊቅጦርት ፡ ወፊልፅስ ። እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ መስተጋድላን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ አፍራቅያ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ አኃው ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ በሥጋ ፡ ወተጋብኡ" ፡ኅቡረ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅሩ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወኮኑ" ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዳኪዮስ!ቦ ፡ ንጉሥ፤!፤ ፣ ከሓዲ ፡ ዘበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ጐዩ ፡ ፲ደቂቅ ፡ በኤፌሶን!" ፡ ወተኃብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓተ!" ፡ ደብር ።
On this day are the holy spiritual combatants and martyrs Maximus, Numitius, Victor, and Philip. These holy spiritual combatants were of the people of the land of Africa, and they were brothers in the Holy Spirit and not in the flesh. They grouped themselves together for the love of Christ. They lived at the time of Decius, the impious emperor, at the time of whom the seven children of Ephesus fled and hid in a cave in the mountain.
ወእሉሰ ፣ መስተጋድላን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፣ መክሲሞስ ፡ ወአብያጺሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ "ለውእቱ ፡ ንጉሥ፤ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቤኔንኖሙ፤፤ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፤ ተንሥኡ ፡ ኅቡረ! ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ወተሰነአዉ ፡ በ፳ምክር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያግህዱ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ፤ ። ወቀርቡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወከልሑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ፣ ንሕነ፤ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡፤ ከሃደ፤ ፣ ነአምን ፡ 'በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ" ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ወንሰግድ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወናመልኮ፤፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ውእቱ፡፡ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይቅሥፍዎሙ ። ወቀሠፍዎሙ ፡ በጥብጣቤ ፡ ዘአልህምትቶ ፡ ቅሥፈተ ፡ ዓቢየ፤ ። ወዳግመ ፡ ዘበጥዎሙ ፡ በአብትር ፡ ወአውዓዩ ፡ ዘባኖሙቶ ፡ በአብትርተ ፡ ሐዩን ፡፣ 'ዘአንደድዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ። ወእምዝ ፡ መዝመዙ ፡ 'ቀሩኑስላተ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ” ፡ በአጽርቅተ ፡ "ፀጐረ ፡ ሠቅ" ፡ እንዘ ፣ ርሑሳት ፤ በብሒእ ፡ ወበፄው፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢሰ ምዑ ፤ ትእዛዞ ፡ ወኢፈርሁ ፡፤ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ ወኢተመይጡ ፡ እምክሮሙ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወተቁጥዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአዘዘ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎሙ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወገ ብሩ ፡ ቦሙቶ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ። ወብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በህየ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ትእግሥቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብ ሐት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ 'ርእሶ ፡ ለመንፈቆሙ ፡ ወይገምድዎሙ ?" ፡ ለመን[ፈ]ቆሙ" ፡ በአስይፍት ፡ በበምታር ፡ ወበዝንቱ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡
These spiritual combatants and martyrs, Maximus and his companions, when they saw the impious emperor torturing the Christians, rose together, gathered, and agreed with one mind to publish their faith. They came before the impious emperor and cried out, saying: 'We are Christians openly; we believe in our Lord Christ, Son of the living God, and we worship and serve him.' When the impious one heard this speech, he became angry against them and ordered them to be scourged, and they were scourged with ox-leather straps with a great scourging; they were struck again with sticks; their backs were burned with iron bars that had been heated in the fire. Then, the wounds of their bodies were rubbed with rags of woolen cloth moistened with vinegar and salt. As they did not obey his order, nor fear his torture, nor turn away from their beautiful sentiment, the emperor became very angry and ordered them to undergo great torture, and it was done to them as he had ordered. Many of those who stood there, when they saw the constancy of the holy martyrs, believed in our Lord Christ—glory to him! Immediately, the emperor ordered the head of half of them to be cut off and the other half to be cut up with swords


[p. 240] {#po.44.p240}
ኩሎሙ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
piece by piece. By this, they all received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ካሌብ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጸድቅ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፪ለኅዳር ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ዘተሠይመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፣ ጢሞቴዎስ ። ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖጥል ቆሙ ፡ ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳ወ፪፳ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ 'ኀረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፤ ለሚመተ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበር ፣ ሐዋርያዊ ፤ ጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አካክዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ቀኑስጥንጥንያ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትአመን ፣ ውስቴታ ፡ በሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ቄርሎስ ፡ 'ወለቅዱስ ፡ ዲዮስቆሮስ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መልእክት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል" ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትፈለጥ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ እምትስብእቱ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተዋሕዶቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይሠናዓር ፡ ረባሕ ፡ በተዋሕዶት ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎቱ!" ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ መልእክቲሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይ[ት]ወከፎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃ[ይ]ማኖቱ ፡ ርትዕት ። ወፈነዋ ፡ ለይ[እ]ቲ ፡ መልእክት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፻ኤሏስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ማእምራን ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ቀኑስጥንጥንያ ፡ ወተወክፎሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ ወተወክፈ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ዘምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወተሳተፎሙ ፡ በቅዳሴ ፡ ዮርባን፤፤ ፣ ወአንበባ፤ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ መልእ ክት ፡ በቅድመ፤ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ በቃሉ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ 'ካልአ ፡ መልእክተ! ፡ ወፈነዋ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡፣ ውስቴታ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ቃላት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ እመጻሕፍት ፡ ቅዱሳት ። 'ወአስተጋብኦሙ ፡ አባ፤ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ለኤ ኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወአንበባ ፡ ለይ[እ]ቲ ፤ መልእክት ፣፡ ቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ተፈ ሥሑ ፡፣ ባቲ! ፡ ፈድፋደ ። 'ወአደሞሙ ፡ ቃላቲሃ! ፡ ወፍካሬሃ ፡ ወተአመኑ ፡ "በሱታፌሁ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፤ ፡ በሃይማኖት?” ፡ ርትዕት ። ወበጽሐ ፡ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ዓቢይ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዓላዊያን ፡ ወአረማዊያን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወሰደድዎ ፡ እመንበረ ፤ ሚመቱ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ወነበረ፡፤ ፡ ውስተ?፥፤፡ ፡ ሚመቱ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይምህሮሙ?፥ ፡ ለመርዔቱ ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ርትዕት ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ርሑቅ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ስደት ፡ኮነ፤ ይፈኑ፤ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ወይምህሮሙ2" ፣ ከመ ፡ ይጽንዑ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት፤ ፡ በመልእክታቲሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ኮነ፥0 ፡ ይሜህሮሙ ፡
On this day also died Caleb, the righteous king. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen. [READING] OF 2 HEDAR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Abba Peter, patriarch of the city of Alexandria, who was instituted after the holy Timothy. Among the patriarchs, he is the twenty-seventh. After God had chosen this holy father for the patriarchal office and he had sat on the apostolic seat, the holy Acacius, patriarch of the city of Constantinople, wrote him a letter confessing therein the holy Trinity, which is one God, according to the faith of the holy Cyril and the holy Dioscorus. He made an exposition in this letter in these terms: 'It is not fitting to separate his [the Son's] divinity from his human nature after their union, under pain of suppressing the benefit that is in the union.' Abba Peter wrote him in response to his letter, accepting therein what was concerning his [Acacius's] right faith, and he sent this letter with three learned bishops. When they arrived before the patriarch of the city of Constantinople, he received these bishops and received the letter they had with them, associated them in the consecration of the eucharist, and read the letter before the people who believed in his word. Then he wrote another letter and sent it to Abba Peter, the explanation of many words of the holy Scriptures being written therein. Abba Peter gathered his bishops and read this letter before them; they rejoiced much in it, its terms and its explanation pleased them, and they confessed that he [Acacius] was in union with them in the right faith. A great misfortune happened to this father from the impious and the pagans because of the faith. He was driven from the seat of his office, and, a short time after, he returned and remained in his office. He instructed his flock without ceasing and fortified it in the right faith. When he was far from it in exile, he sent it letters and instructed it, so that it would be firm in the right faith, by his letters. When he was with it, he

በቃላቲሁ፤ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ፳ዓመተ። ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ አባ ፡ ሰኑትዩ ፡ "ወውእቱ ፡ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ለሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳትቶ፥ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ፳ወ፻ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይምህሮሙ0?፥" ፡ ለሕዝቡ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ 'ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሚመቱ፥ ፲ወ፳ ዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝካራ ፡ ለሴቴንዋ ፡ ነቢያዊት ፡ ዘእስክንድርያ ፡ ወቅድስት ፡ አንስጣስያ ፡ ወአባ ፣ ሊባኖስ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። አመ ፡ ጀለኅዳር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ! ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ፤ ፡ ኪራኮስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ኮነ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቆሮንቶስ ፡ "ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ርቱ ዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወመሀርዎ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን" ፡ ወአቅረብዎ ፡ ኅበ ፣ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ቆሮንቶስ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ ወሜሞ ፡ አናጐንስጢስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እኅወ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወትረ፣፡ ያነብብ ፤ መጸሕፍተ ፡ ወያሐትት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፣ ቃላቲሆሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ሥርዓት!" ፡ ወሕገ፤፤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወተለዓለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ በትምህርቱ ፡ ወበጥበቡ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ 'ይኤዝዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይኅድግ፤" ፡ አንብቦ ፤ መጻሕ ፍተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያነብብ ፡ 'መጻሕፍተ ፡ ወትረ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፣፤ ወበውስተ?፤ ፣ ቤቱ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፤ በአንብቦቱ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፲ወ፳ቿዓመተ ፤ ሰአልዎ ፡ አበዊሁ ፤ ከመ ፡ ያስተዋስብዎ! ፣ ወውእቱስ ፡፤ ኢፈቀደ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ኃሠሠ ፡ እምአበዊሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድግዎ ፡ ይሖር"" ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፳እምነ ፣ አድባራት ፡ ወኀደግዎ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወይትመየጥ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አበዊሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ በዝኃ ፡ ሑረቱ ፡!ኀበ ፡ አድባራት፤ ፡ ፈተወ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይልበስ ፡ አልባሰ ፣ ምንኩስና ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ! ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ቅድስት ፣ ወተጋብአ፡ ፡ በቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ዚአሃ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አልባሰ፤ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዘአፍቀርከ፡ ፡ ወተነበየ ፡
instructed it by his words. He remained eight years on the seat of his office and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy father, the patriarch Abba Schenoudi. Among the patriarchs of the city of Alexandria, he is the sixty-third. This father instructed his people and remained on the seat of his office fifteen years, and he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also is the commemoration of Setenna, the prophetess of Alexandria, and of the holy Anastasia and of Abba Libanos. May their blessing be with our king Iyasu for ever and ever.] [READING] OF 3 HEDAR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy and great father Cyriacus. This spiritual combatant was by his birth of the people of the city of Corinth, and his parents were Christians of right faith. They taught him the teaching of the Church and brought him before Abba Peter, bishop of the city of Corinth; he prayed over him and instituted him as reader, for he [Cyriacus] was the son of his father's brother. Then, he read the Scriptures constantly and sought the interpretation of their words as well as the ordinance and the law of the Church. He rose above many men by his science and by his wisdom. The bishop ordered him not to cease reading the Scriptures, and he read the Scriptures constantly at the church for the people and in the house of the bishop. The bishop rejoiced that he read the Scriptures. When his days were eighteen years, his parents asked him to marry, but he did not want that. After that, he asked his parents to allow him to go to one of the monasteries of the land; they allowed him. He went constantly to the monasteries and returned to his parents. When he had gone many times to the monasteries, he desired to clothe himself with the holy monastic habit; he went to the holy city of Jerusalem and met with the holy Abba Cyril, the bishop thereof, and explained to him his thought concerning the monastic habit. Cyril said to him:
ላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይከውን ፡ አበ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወይትጋደል ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ብዙኃ፤፡ ወይበርሁ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ ብዙኃን ። 'ወእምዝ ፡ ባረኮ ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ አባ ፡ ሮማኖስ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔተ፤" ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ዘፍልስጥኤም ። ወተወክፎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወአልቦሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስ[ና]። ወመጠዎ ፡ ለ፳እምነ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይገሥጾ ፡ ወይምሀሮ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወያጠይቆ ፡ ምክንያቶ" ፡ ለሰይጣን ። ወተጸምደ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ወበሰጊድ ፡ ወበትጋህ ፡ ወበጻማ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡'በትሕትና ፡ ወበትዕግሥት፥፤ ፡ ወበየውሃት ። ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ድውያን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይፈውሶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ድውያን ፡ እለ ፡ ይበጽሑ፥ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወተሰምዓ ፡ ዜና ፡ ትሩፋቲሁ" ፣፡ ወቅድስናሁ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መካን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፻፪ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ቱስጥንጥንያ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መቅዶንዮስ ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ ቅዱስ፥ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ወተቃረኖ ፡ ለመቅዶንዮስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወሞኦ ፡ በቃለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘየኃድር፤ ፣ ውስቴቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ በርሥእና ፣ ሠናይት ፣፤ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወእ[ም]ድኅረ ፡ ፅረፍቱ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እ[ም]ሥጋሁ ፤ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ= ። ወእምኔሆሙ+ ፡ እስመ ፣ ሥጋሁቶ፤ ፡ ይነብር ፡ በ፳እምነ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወእምጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ "ወእስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ኢይማስን ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ወኩሎሙ ‹፤ እለ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወይሬእዩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ የሐልዩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ እምቅሩብ፣ መዋዕል ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አዕረፈ፥" ፡ እመጠነ ፡ ፪፻ዓመት ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ 'ኮነ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ፤፤ ፡ ለቴዮዶስዮስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፤ አቡሆሙ ፡ ለአርቃድዮስ ፡ ወአኖርዮስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኪራኮስ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡
'It is a beautiful thing that you love.' He prophesied concerning him that he would be an illustrious father, would wage many spiritual combats, and that, through him, the souls of many men would shine. Then Abba Cyril blessed him and sent him to the venerable father Abba Romanus, superior of the monks of Palestine. The latter welcomed him, rejoiced in him, and clothed him with the monastic habit. He entrusted him to one of the elders of the monastery so that he might admonish him, teach him the monastic way, and indicate to him the ruses of Satan. This saint devoted himself to asceticism in fasting and prayer, in adoration and in vigil, in great labor day and night, in humility, in continence, and in gentleness. God gave him the gift of healing the sick: he healed all the sick who came to him. The report of his virtues and his holiness was heard in every place. When the holy Abba Cyril, bishop of the city of Jerusalem, went to the holy council of the hundred and fifty bishops who gathered in the city of Constantinople concerning Macedonius, the enemy of the Holy Spirit, the holy Cyril took this saint with him to the council; he fought the impious Macedonius and defeated him by the word of the Holy Spirit that dwelt in him. When he was advanced in a beautiful old age and had pleased God, he died in peace. After his death, God manifested from his body many miracles and wonders. Among these is the following: his body remained in one of the monasteries of the city of Jerusalem, and from the moment of his death until now, his body has not altered. All those who go to the city of Jerusalem and see his body think that he died a few days ago, but he died about seven hundred years ago; indeed, it was in the time of the Emperor Theodosius the Great, the father of Arcadius and Honorius. May the prayer and the blessing of this saint Cyriacus be with us, amen.


[p. 244] {#po.44.p244}
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ አትናስዮስ ፡ ወኢራኢ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ኩነኖሙ ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡ እምኩነኔሆሙ ፡ ገደፎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግብ ፡ ዕራቁ፤ ፡ ወፈጸመ፥፤ ፡ ኖኅቶ ፡ዲቤሆሙ" ፡ ወአዕ[ረ]ፉ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ። <ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ነአኩቶ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ወተፋቅሮ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ስእለ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ለዓለመ ፤ ዓለም ።>
On this day died also the holy Athanasius and Irene his sister after Maximian had made them undergo great torture. When he was weary of torturing them, he had them thrown into an empty cistern and had the lid closed over them, and they died there. May God have mercy on us by their prayer, amen. <On this day also died Na'akueto-la-'Ab, the king of peace and love. May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear Se'ela Seyon for ever and ever.>

አቡሁ ፡ በእደ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ይስድዎ ፡ አትሮንሰ ፡ ማርያም ፤
[On this day also died the holy Amda Mika'el, the leader, the pillar of the kings of Ethiopia who are Zara Ya'qob, Ba-eda Maryam, and Eskender. The name of the father of this saint was Claudius and the name of his mother, Elisabeth. They were rich in goods and in faith. They begot the saint during the feast of the four Animals and called him by the name of Amda Mika'el. After he was born, the year of his birth, he entered crawling into the interior of the ark-vessel of the church of the saint Michael and remained there three days without anyone knowing it. His parents thought he was dead and remained weeping for him. Then, while the priest was looking for incense inside the ark, he found the little child sitting there without fear, and there were no excrements on the ground. His parents took him from there and rejoiced greatly to have found him alive again. Then, he grew in wisdom and in science so much that the kings made him leader and governor of all that concerned their house. He was a father to the poor by his justice and by his alms; he renovated all the churches with his money; the violent and iniquitous people disappeared in his time; he destroyed all the cities of the pagans, which were on every side, by his fasting and his prayer and with the help of the saints, so that they submitted to the domination of the kings of Ethiopia and paid them tribute. As for this saint Amda Mika'el, his heart was in the love of God, and he did not take his meal before having invoked the names of the saints and before having drunk water consecrated by prayer, and he did not pass a day without plunging himself into water consecrated by prayer. He spent all his fortune in alms. When God saw his virtue, he prepared for him the salvation of his soul: iniquitous people accused him before the king to kill him; but the king spared him for he loved him much, and, as they pressed him with their speeches, he had him chained against his will and exiled him to a distant land where he was in great peril. Michael the archangel helped him, brought him bread and a cup so that he might eat and drink, and promised him the Garden of delights. When Amda Mika'el wanted to receive communion, the archangel brought it down to him from heaven and administered it to him. Then those who were his accusers deliberated, had him brought to the king's court, and killed him. That night, a God-fearing monk went to see his corpse and found three monks incensing his body. He told it, saying and swearing by our Lord Jesus Christ that he was not lying about that. Some saw a luminary descending from heaven upon him, the body, for several days. As for the king, when he remembered his virtue, he cursed those who had killed him, had a proclamation made by a herald, and ordered him, Amda Mika'el, to be buried with honor in the tomb of his fathers. He instituted a commemoration for him, and as for his children, he honored and loved them much. Then, when the king Lebna Dengel learned the pact made concerning this by his father


[p. 246] {#po.44.p246}
ወበህየ ፡ ቀበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መቃብረ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ወስመ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
Ba-eda Maryam who dwelt with him, he ordered the body


[p. 248] {#po.44.p248}
to be taken to Atronsa Maryam and, there, he had him interred in the tomb of the kings so that there might be a commemoration and a name for him. May God have mercy by his prayer on our king Iyasu for ever and ever.]

### READING OF 4 HEDAR
አመ ፡ 8ለኅዳር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡፣ አቢማኮስ ፡ ወአዛርያኖስ ። ወእሉ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወአስተዋደይዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዘተሠይመ ፡ እምኅበ! ፡ መክስምያኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ 'እሙንቱ ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ምእመናን ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ተስእሎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ ዘለፍዎ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ በእንተ ፡ 'ዘኃደጎ ፡ ለአምላክ ፡ ዘፈጠረ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘውስቴቶሙ ፡ ወአምለከ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ እለ ፡ ግቡራን ፡ በእደ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይሬእዩ ፡ ወኢይሰምዑ ፡ ወኃደረ ፤ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ስይጣን ፡ ወአስሐተ ፡ ሰብአ ። ወአንከረ ፡ መኩንን ፡ እምተሐብሎቶሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ክሳውዲሆሙ ፡'ወመተሩ፣፤ ርእሶሙ ፡ በሰይፍ፤ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ትንብልናሆሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day are the holy martyrs Epimachus and Azarianus. They were of the city of Rome. They were accused before a governor who had been appointed by Maximian of being faithful to Christ. When they arrived before him, he questioned them concerning their faith, and they confessed that they were Christians; then they reprimanded the governor because he had abandoned God who created the heaven and the earth and all that is therein, and because he worshipped idols that were made by the hand of men, which neither saw nor heard and in which dwelt Satan who deceived men. The governor was astonished at their boldness and ordered their necks to be cut. Their heads were cut off with the sword, and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May their intercession be with our king Iyasu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለሳቦር" ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሐርምዝ" ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ 'ሶበ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግዱ ፡ ለፀሐይ!" ፡ ወለእሳት ፡ ወይሥዑ ፡ ሎሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢሰምፁ ፡ ትእዛዞ ፡ አዘዘ፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎሙ ፡ 'ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ፤።'፤፤ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ይምህርዎሙ፤ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወያጸንዕዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡፤ እግዚእነ፤፤ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ንጉሥ፤ ፡ ጽንዖሙ፤ ፡ ወትዕግሥቶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ኩነኔ ፡ ወኢተመይጡ ፤ እምሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ ወኢኀደጉ ፡ ትምህርቶሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ!፤ ፡ አዘዘ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግድፍዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡እሳት ፡ ወገደፍዎሙ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወመጠዉ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚ![እ]ነ”' ፡ ክርስቶስ፤፤ ። ወነሥኡ፡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን፤ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡
On this day the saints John and James, bishops of the land of Persia, became martyrs by the hand of Sapor, son of Hormuz the king of Persia, when he ordered them to worship the sun and the fire and to sacrifice to them. As they did not obey his order, he ordered them to undergo great torture. While they were under torture, they taught the people and fortified them in the faith of our Lord Christ—glory to him! When the king saw their firmness and their constancy in the torture and that they did not turn away from their faith nor cease their teaching to the people, he ordered them to be thrown into the fire; they were thrown into it immediately, and they surrendered their soul into the hand of our Lord Christ and received the crown of martyrdom with all the saints. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ደማስቆ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለንጉሠ ፡ ዓረብ ፡ ተንበላት ። 'እስመ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ሶርያ ፤ ወብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ 'ተዋሥአ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ 8እምነ ፡ ማእምራኒሆሙ ፡ ወሞኦ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአጽንዖ፤ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከመ” ፡
On this day also the saint Thomas, bishop of the city of Damascus, became a martyr by the hand of the king of the Muslim Arabs. Indeed, when the Muslims reigned over the land of Syria and the land of Egypt, this saint disputed with one of their doctors: this saint


[p. 250] {#po.44.p250}
እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈጣሬ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘውስቴቶሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተመውአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ዚአሆሙቶ፤ ፡ ወአስተዋደዮ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ክርስቲያ ናዊ ፤ ረገመ ፡ ሃይማኖተነ ። ወአብጽሖ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፥ ፡ ወተስ እሎ ፡፤ ወይቤሎ ፡፣ ቦኑ፤ ፡ ረገምከ ፡ ሃይማኖተነ፥ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ፥" ፡ ዘንቱጾ ፡ በእንቲአከ ። ወይቤሎቶ፥ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መርገምሰ ፡ ኢወጽአ ፡ እምአፉየ ፡ አላ ፡ አነ ፡ "አጽናዕኩ ፣፤ ሎቱ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፤ አምላክ። ፡ ዘበአማን ፤ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕጉ ፡፤ ለክርስቶስ ፤ ኢይመጽእ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ሕግ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወሕገ ፡ ዚአነሰ ፡ በኀቤከ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አው ፡ አልቦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኢኮነ ፣፡ ሕግክሙ+ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ" ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ 'ላዕሌሁ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ። ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡
vanquished him and proved to him that our Lord Christ is God, creator of heaven and earth and of all that is in them. When the Muslim had been vanquished, he went to their (= the Muslims') prince, denounced the holy Thomas, and said to him (= the prince): "This Christian has cursed our faith." The prince had this saint brought, questioned him, and said to him: "Have you cursed our faith, as this man has said about you?" The saint said to him: "As for cursing, it has not come out of my mouth, but I have proved to him that Christ is true God and that, after the law of Christ, no other law would come." The prince said to him: "Does our law, according to you, come from God or not?" The saint said to him: "Your law does not come from God." When he said this, the prince became angry with the saint and ordered his head to be cut off. His head was cut off, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May his prayer and blessing be with us, amen.
የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ 'ለአብ ፡ ዓቢይ፤። ፡ ዘካርያስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረ ከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአባ ፣ አበይዶ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ዘመነነ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ረድአ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐኒ ፡ ወጉባኤ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ወጴጥሮስ ፡ ሊቀ፣ ጳጳሳት፥ ፡ ዘእለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፤ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለሐመልማላ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሮማነ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወለተ ፡ ናኦድ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወሰማዕት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also is the commemoration of the illustrious father Zakäryäs (= Zacharias). May his prayer and blessing be with us, amen. [On this day also is the commemoration of abbä 'Abaydo (= Abaidus), the accomplished monk who despised the way of life of this world, the disciple of abbä Yohanni (= John), and of a multitude of martyrs and of Pêtros (= Peter), patriarch of Alexandria. May their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.] [On this day also is the death of Hamalmälä warg, son of Romäna warg, daughter of Nä'od, king of Ethiopia. May God have mercy on him through the prayer of all the saints and martyrs for ever and ever, amen.]
አመ ፡ ፳ለኅዳር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡፣ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ አስተርአየት፤ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዓቢይ፤ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ፡ ባዕለ ፡ ኩናት ፡ ዘረገዞ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ መድኃኒነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ስቁል ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፅፀ ፣፡ መስቀል ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጢባርዮስ ፡ ቄሣር ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀጳዶቅያ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ” ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ፻ ለወርኃ ፡ ሐምሌ ። ወኮነት ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፤ ወሥጋሁ ፡ ግዱፋን ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ እምኅበ ፡፤ ንጉሥ ፡
[READING] FOR THE 5TH OF HEDAR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day appeared the head of the great saint Langinos (= Longinus), the possessor of the lance that pierced the side of Christ our Savior while he was crucified on the wood of the cross. This happened thus: when the emperor Tibäryos Qésär (= Tiberius Caesar) dispatched to the land of Qapadoqyä (= Cappadocia) and had the head of the saint cut off on the twenty-third of the month of

ወመተረ ፡ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ርእሶ፤፤ ፡ ለ[ቅ]ዱስ፤። ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ኀበ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም ፤ ወመጠዋ ፡ ለሏላጦስ ፡ ጳንጤናዊ ። ወሏላጦስኒ"" ፡ አርአየ፤ ፡ ርእሶ! ፡ ለአይሁድ፤! ፣ ወተፈሥሑ?” ፡ በሞቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ፤ ፡ ኢላጦስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅብርዉ ፡ 'ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳አውግር ፡ ዘአፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡፤ ኢየሩሳ ሌም"! ፡ ወቀበርዋ፤። ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኮነት ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቀጳዶቅያ ፡ አምነት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰበከ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ቀጳዶቅያ ። ወሶበ ፡ መተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ኮነት፤ ፡ ትቀውም ፡ በህየ ፡ ወትኔጽር ፡ ወ[ት]በኪ ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ዖሩ ፡ አዕይንቲሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ብእ ሲት ። ወነሥአት ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ፤ ፡ ትትባረክ፡ ፡ እ ምነ ፡ መካናት ፡ ቅዱሳት፤"” ፡ ወትሰግድ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ" ፡ ወትተነብል ፡ በመቃብረ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዮጊ ፡ ይትከሠቱ ፡ አዕይንቲሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሞተ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ወተወሰከ ፡ ሐዘና ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሞቱ፥፤ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዑረተ ፡ አዕይንቲሃ ። ወሐጥዓት ፡ ዘያበጽሓ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔራ ፡ ወበከየትቶ" ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ፡ ወኖመት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተሐዝን ። ወርእየቶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ፡ በንዋም ፡ ወምስሌሁቶ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ዘሞተ ። ወይቤላ ፡ ቅዱስ፥፤ ፡ ሖሪ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካነ ፡ እገሌ ፡ ወንሥኢ ፡ርእስየ ፡ እምህየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሀት ፡ እምንዋማ ፡ ተስእለት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወአብጽሕዋ ፡ ኅቤሁቶ ። ወሶበ ፡ 'ከረየት ፡ ውእተ ፡ መካነቶ፥ ፡ ዓርገ ፡'ላቲ፣፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡ ብዙኅቶ፥ ፡ ዘጹናሁ ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለቅዱስ፥ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ላቲ ፡ እምኔሃ፤ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዓቢይ። ። "ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተከ ሥቱ ፡ አፅይንቲሃ ፡ ወርእየት ፡ ሠናየ ። ወሰብሐቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአፍለሰት ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወአንበረት ፡ ዕፍረተ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ ወረሰየታ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለወ ልዳ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔራ ፡እንዘ ፡ ትሴብሖ ፡ ለክርስቶስ፥ ፡ ወትወድሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ለንጊኖስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ወ]ፍልሰተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ሊቀ" ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሰጥብ ፡ "ውስተ ፡ ደወሉ” ፡ ለ]እስዩጥ ፡ በላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወቅዳሴ ፥ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ተኣምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ዓበይት! ። ወተዝካረ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐኒ]። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
hamilë, the head of the saint and his body were abandoned. The soldier who had come on behalf of the emperor and had cut off the head of the saint took the head of the saint, carried it with him to Jerusalem, and handed it over to Pilätos Panténäawi (= Pontius Pilate). Pilate showed the head to the Jews, and they rejoiced at his (= Longinus') death. Then Pilate ordered the head of the saint to be buried in a hill that was outside the city of Jerusalem, and it was buried. Many days later, it happened that a woman from the land of Cappadocia had believed through the ministry of the saint Langinos when he was preaching in the land of Cappadocia, and when his head was cut off, she stood there, watching and weeping over him. By the will of God—glory to Him!—the eyes of this woman became blind. She took her son and went to Jerusalem to be blessed by the Holy Places, to prostrate herself there, and to ask for the intercession of the tomb of our Lord Christ in the hope that her eyes might see again. When she arrived in Jerusalem, her son died; her grief redoubled because of his death and because of the blindness of her eyes. She had no one who could bring her back to her country; she shed bitter tears and fell asleep while being afflicted. She saw the saint Langinos in her sleep, and he had with him her son who had died. The saint said to her: "Go to such and such a place and take my head from there." When the woman awoke from her sleep, she inquired about the place, and they brought her there. When she had dug in that place, an abundant perfume rose toward her, the odor of which was very sweet. When she reached the head of the saint, a great light appeared to her, and immediately her eyes opened and she saw well. She praised God, moved the head of the saint, put perfume on it, placed it with the body of her son, and went away to her country, glorifying Christ and praising the saint Langinos. May his prayer and blessing be with us, amen. On this day also is [the commemoration of the saint Timotëwos (= Timothy) the martyr and] the translation of the body of the saint Téwodros (= Theodore), the martyr and army commander, to the city of Sateb (= Chatab) in the district of 'Esyut (= Assiout) in Upper Egypt, and the consecration of his church. Great miracles and wonders occurred from his body [and there is the commemoration of abbä Yohanni (= John)]. May his prayer and blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.
አመ ፡ ሄለኅዳር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ፈልኮስ" ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ወመሀርዎ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ አንስጥያዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ዲያቆነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ግዕዞ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወጽድቆ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለቴዎናስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ኀረይዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፊልኮስ፤" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ወተሠይመ ፡ በፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወዓቀበ ፡ መርዔቶ ፡ 'ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤፤ ፡ በሠናይ ፡ ተዓቅቦ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ትሩስ፤፤ ፡ ቄሳር ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ቄሣር ፤ አመንደቦሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፡ እለ ፡ የአምኑ፤" ፡ "በእግዚእነ ፣ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ ኩነኔያተ ፡ ዕፁባተ፤ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወብዙኃን ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታተ ፡ በእደዊሁ ። ወበጽሖ!" ፡ ለዝ ንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወሐዘን፤ ። ወአስተብቀዓ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፤ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፤ ወአጥፍኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤፤ ፡ በዳግም፤ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለክርስቲ ያን ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይኩንኖሙ፤ ። ወጸለየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ" ፡ ፊልኮስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ኢያርእዮ ፡ ኩነኔሆሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ። ወአዕረፈ ፡ 'በቀዳሚ ፡ መዋዕል" ፡ ዓመተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ። ወኩሉ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ "ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበር ፡ ሐዋርያዊ ፡ ፳ዓመት ፡ ወመንፈቅ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ደረሰ ፤ ድርሳናተ ፣ ወተግሣጻተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። 'ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ተግሣጸጻተ ፤ ወትምህር ታተ ፡ ወቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ተዋሥአ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግዘት" ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡
[READING] FOR THE 6TH OF HEDAR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Filkos (= Felix), patriarch of the city of Rome. The parents of this saint were Christians and instructed him in all the science of the Church. 'Anesteyäyos (= Anastasius), patriarch of the city of Rome, ordained him deacon. When Yostos (= Justus) was elected patriarch of the city of Rome, he saw the good nature of this father and his righteousness and ordained him priest. When the father Diyonäsyos (= Dionysius), patriarch of the city of Rome, who lived in the time of Tëwonäs (= Theonas), patriarch of the city of Alexandria, died, this saint Filkos was chosen to be patriarch of the city of Rome, and he was instituted by the will of God. He guarded the flock of our Lord Christ with a good guard. When Terus Qésär (= Probus Caesar) died and Téwodros Qésär (= Theodore Caesar) reigned after him, he persecuted the faithful who believed in our Lord Christ with a great persecution and made them undergo very painful torments; many of them became martyrs by his hands. Through him, great torments and afflictions came to this father. This father begged God concerning him, and God caused him to perish in the second year of his reign. When Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) the infidel reigned, he persecuted the Christians and began to torture them. This father Filkos prayed to God not to let him see the torture of the Christians, and he died in the first days of the first year of the reign of Diocletian. All the days that this father remained on the apostolic seat were five years and a half. He died after having composed many homilies and sermons; among them are sermons and teachings, and there are some that deal with excommunication and the right faith; they are very good and are useful to the people of the Christians. May his prayer and blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 254] {#po.44.p254}
ርትዕፅት ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ሠናያን ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣ ያበቀዑ ፤ ለሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ጸሎቱ ፡፤ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተጋብአ ፡ 'መድኃኒነ ፡ ክርስቶስ" ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ፤ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ዮስቋም ፡ ወቀደሰ ፡ ታቦቶ ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኖ ፡ ወሰርዓ ፡ 'ቅዳሴ ፡ ቀዮርባን። ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያቲሁ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወውእቱ ፡ ቀዳሜ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቆፉስቋም ፡ በከመ ፡'ኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቴዎፊሎስ ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also our Savior Christ met with his disciples on Mount Quesquâm (= Qosqam), consecrated his tabernacle and his church, and established the consecration of the Eucharist with his apostles on this day. This was the first consecration—on Mount Qosqam—as the saint Téwofilos (= Theophilus) and the saint Qérellos (= Cyril), patriarchs of the city of Alexandria, are witnesses of this. May their prayer and their blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen.


[p. 256] {#po.44.p256}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡፣ ካዕበ ፡፣ ኮነ ፣ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአፍሮስ ፤ ወአንዴዎስ?" ፡ ወይስጦስ ፡ ወባርትሮስ፤ ፡ ወሉኪሮስ ፡፣ 'ወሳውኖስ ፣፤ ወለንዲሮስ፥ ፡ ወኡላስዮስ ፡ ወቆርኔሌዎስ፥" ፡ ፻፻ወ ድ፻ማኅበራነ ፣ ኤስድሮስ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ሂለኅዳር ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ እስክንድርያዊ ፡ ሰማዕት፤ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አቡሁ ፤ ነጋዴ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወልደ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ 'በበዓለ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቤተ ፡፤ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ልዳ ፡፤ አመ ፡ ለወርኃ ፡ ኅዳር ። ወሰአሎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ከመ፣ ይተንብል ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ ወልደ ። ወተወክፈ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእለቶ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወሰመዮ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ። ወእሙ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እኅቱ ፡ ለአርማንዮስ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወአዕረፉ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ወነበረ" ፡ ኀበ ፡ እሃ ፡ ለእሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፳ወ ፳ዓመት ። ወኮነ ፡ ያፈቅሮሙ ፡ለነዳያን ፡'ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መሓሬ ፡ ወር፤፤ ፡ ወያፈቅር ፡ ቤተ፣ ክርስቲያነ ። ወኮነ፤ ፡ ለአርማንዮስ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ ባሕቲታ ። ወወፅአት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ ምስለ ፡ አብያጺሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትፌሣሕ ፡ ወረከበት ፡ ደብረ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውስቴታ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ኅቡኣን፤ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይዜምሩ ፡ 'ዝማሬ ፡ ጥዑመ! ፡ ወቦአ!፤ ፡ ዝማሬሆሙ ፡ ወስብሐታቲሆሙ ፡ ወተተክለ፤ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ልባ፤" ። ወአኀዘት ፡ ትስእሎ ፡ ለጊዮርጊስ፤ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እኅተ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቃ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝማሬ ፡ ዘኮኑ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ይዜምሩ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወአኀዘ ፡ ያጠይቃ ፡ ወያለብዋ ፡ ትርጓሜ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝማሬ ፡ ወነገራ ፡ ዘይበጽሖሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ፡ እምኩ ነኔ! ፡ ወዘይበጽሖሙ” ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ወለጌራን ፡ 'እምነ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወዕረፍት፤፤ ። "ወሶበ ፣ ተመይጠት፡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ አርማንዮስ ፡ ተአምነት ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ በእግዚ እነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይቲጣ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኢትግበሪ ፡ 'ዘንተ ፡ ኦወለትየ ፡ ወኢሰምዓት ፡ ነገሮ ፤ ወኢተአዘዘት ፡ ሎቱ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእላ ፡ በሰይፍ ፣ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወነገርዎ ፡ ለመኩንን ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እኅትከ ፡ ውእቱ"" ፡ ዘአስ ሐታጾ ። ወአኀዞ ፡ 'ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ 'ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ፥" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እንጽና ፡ ወኩነንዎ ፡ በህየ ፡ 'ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ፥፤ ። ወእምድኅረዝ፤ ፡ መተርዎ፥" ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቅድስተ ፣ በሰይፍ ፤ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡
[On this day also is the commemoration of 'Afros, 'Andëwos, Yestos (= Justus), Bärteros, Lukiros, Säwnos, and of Nediros, 'Uläsyos, Qornëlëwos (= Cornelius), and the seventeen thousand companions of 'Ésderos (= Isidore). May their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever, amen.] [READING] FOR THE 7TH OF HEDAR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day the saint Giyorgis (= George), the Alexandrian, became a martyr. The father of this saint was a merchant of the people of the city of Alexandria; he had no son. He went to the feast of the consecration of the church of the saint Giyorgis the martyr in the city of Ledä (= Lydda), on the seventh of the month of Hedär, and he asked the saint Giyorgis to intercede for him with God so that He might give him a son. God accepted his request and gave him a son; he called him Giyorgis. The mother of this saint was the sister of 'Armänyos (= Armenius), the governor of the city of Alexandria. His parents died, and he remained with his mother's brother. His days were twenty-five years; he loved the poor very much; he was compassionate, virtuous, and loved the Church. 'Armänyos had an only daughter. This girl went out with her companions to amuse herself and met a monastery outside the city of Alexandria. There were hidden monks there; they were singing sweet chants. Their chant and their hymns entered and took root in her heart. She began to question Giyorgis, the son of her father's sister, so that he might teach her the explanation of this chant that the monks were singing. He began to teach her and make her understand the explanation of this chant and told her what would happen to sinners in terms of punishment and what would happen to the just and the virtuous in terms of joy and rest. When the young girl returned to her father 'Armänyos, she confessed our Lord Christ before him. He undertook to persuade her and said to her: "Do not do this, my daughter." She did not listen to his words and did not obey him. He ordered her head to be cut off with the sword, and she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. They spoke to the governor and said to him: "It is Giyorgis, the son of your sister, who led her astray." He had this saint Giyorgis seized and made him undergo great torture. Then, he sent him to the city of 'Ensenä (= Antinoë), and they made him undergo great torture there. After that, they cut off his holy head with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. There was a deacon there whose name was Samu'el (= Samuel); he took his holy body


[p. 258] {#po.44.p258}
ስምፅ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወሀሎ ፡ በህየ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ መንፍስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ለአርማንዮስ ፡ እጐሃ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፣ ፈነወት ፡ ወነሥአት ፤ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወረሰየቶ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ወለታ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
and carried it to the city of Manfes (= Memphis). When the wife of 'Armänyos, the brother of the mother of the saint Giyorgis, knew that Giyorgis had become a martyr, she sent to take his body and placed it with the body of her daughter, the martyr, in the city of Alexandria. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ነኅርው ፡ ሰማዕት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ፍዩም ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ለሰማዕታት ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ?" ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሙት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ በራእይ ፡ አንተ ፡ ሀለወከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የሐሊ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እፎ ፡ ይትከሀለኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እብጻሕ፤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ህየ ። ወኮነ ፡ የኀሥሥ ፡ ሐመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹዓን ፡ ዲቤሃ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአኮ ፡ ሚካኤል=፣ ወጾሮ ፡ 'ዲበ ፡ ክነፊሁ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾ ኪያ ፡ ወአቆሞ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ዲዮቅልጥያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ፥ፅ፤ ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፡ "በእግዚእነ ፡ክርስቶስ ። ወተስእሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡!' በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ወሀገሩ፥ ፡ ወአጠየቆ ፡ "ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወአንከረ" ፡ እምጽአቱ ፡ ወጦ ፡ ወተካ የዶ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ 'ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወአልባሳተ ፡ ዲባጋት ፡ ለእመ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዛቲሁ ፤ ወይሠውዕ፥ ፡ ለጣዖታቲሁ ። ወኢሰምዓ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ ወገዘፈ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እኬንነከ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወኢፈ ርሃ" ፡ እምኩነኔሁቶ ፡ ወኢሰምዓ ፡ ት[እ]ዛዛቲሁ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ኩነኔ። ወአሐተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሰደደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ' ፡ አናብስተ ። ወካልአ፤ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አውዓዮ ፡ በእሳት ። ወሣልሰ። ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ኩነንዎ ፡ በመንኩራኩራት ። ወራብዓ ፡ ጊዜ ፣ አብሰልዎ ፡ በእሳት ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ጸሕል ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወሶበ ፤ ደክሙ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ "ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ቅድስተ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወኮነ ፡ ህየንቴሆሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡-እለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡እለ፣ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወከማሁ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ነህርው ፡ኮነ?' ፡ እምድረ፤ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ። ወነበረ" ፡ በህየ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ዩልዮስ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ 'ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ” ፡ ነህርው' ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፪አግብርቲሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ክብር ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
On this day also the saint abbä Nahrew (= Nahroou) became a martyr. This saint was from the land of Feyum (= Fayoum) in the land of Egypt; he feared God greatly. When he learned the story of the martyrs, he went to the city of Alexandria and wanted to die for the name of our Lord Christ. He said to him in a vision: "You must go to the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch)." He reflected in his heart and said: "How is it possible for me to go there?" He was looking for a ship to embark on. God sent him his angel Mikä'ël (= Michael), and he carried him on his wings from the city of Alexandria, brought him to the city of Antioch, and placed him before the emperor Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian). Nahrew confessed our Lord Christ before him. The emperor questioned him about his name and his country, and Nahrew informed him that he was from the land of Egypt. The emperor was astonished at his arrival, caressed him, and promised to give him much money and precious clothes if he obeyed his orders and sacrificed to his idols. But Nahrew did not obey him in what he was saying. His (= Diocletian's) voice became heavy against him, and he said to him: "I am going to make you undergo great torture." Nahrew was not afraid of his torture and did not listen to his orders. The emperor ordered this saint to be tortured by many kinds of torture. Once, the executioner released lions against him; the second time, he burned him with fire; the third time, they tortured him with wheels; the fourth time, they boiled him in fire in a large cauldron. When they were tired of torturing him, the emperor ordered his head to be cut off; they cut off his holy head with the sword, and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. He was in compensation for all the martyrs of the city of Antioch who were martyrs in the land of Egypt; likewise, this saint abbä Nahrew was from the land of Egypt and received the crown of martyrdom in the city of Antioch. The saint Yulyos (= Julius of Aqfahs) was staying there; he took the body of the saint abbä Nahrew and sent it in great honor to his country with two of his servants. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ"" ፡ አባ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆጾስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ትመይ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ”ጾ ፡ ገምኑዲ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡
On this day also died the saint abbä Minäs (= Menas), bishop of the city of Temay (= Thmouis). This saint was from the people of the city of Gamnudi

ለአበዊሁ ። ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ፈራህያነ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይገብሩ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ በጾሞሙ ፣፤ ወበጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበተጸምዶቶሙ ፣ ወተጋድሎቶሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ዜና ፡ ጽድቆሙ፤ ፡ ወጊሩቶሙ" ፡ በኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወበኀበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አበው ። ወአስተዋሰብዎ ፡ ለወልዶሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጸርሑ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተሰምየት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ተሰናአወ ፡ ምስሌሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ደ[ዕ]ቀቡ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ በንጽሕና ፡ ወኢያማስኑ ፡ ድንግልናሆሙ፤ ። "ወኮኑ ፡ ይትጋደሉ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወእምድኅረዝ፤' ፡ ፈተወ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሚናስ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይልበስ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወይቤላ፤፡ ፡ ኢይደልወነ፤” ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ምግባረ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወንሕነ ፡ ንነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ። እስመ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ይለብሱ ፡ ሠቀ ፡ ጸጐር ፡ እምታሕተ ፤ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ ወይቀውሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተግሁ ፡፤ በጸሎት ፡ ወአንብቦ ፡፤ መጻሕፍተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተአ ምኃ ፡ ወአ[ስ]ተፋነወቶ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ" ፡ አባ ፡ እንጦንስ፤የ" ። ወፈቀደ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ይርሐቅ ፡ እምነ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ የኀሥሥዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፤ መካን ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሜጥዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብእሲቱ ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓቀቦ፤፤ ፡ ወኢረከብዎ፤‹" ፡ ግሙራ ። ወነበረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሚናስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸመድ ፡ ወይትጋደል ። ወሀሎ ፡ በህየ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በምንኩስና ፡ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወረደ፤ ፡ እምደብረ፤" ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ፤" ፡ በአስቄጥስ ። ወኮነ ፡ "ውእቱ ፡ በመዋዕሊሆሙ!" ፥ ለ፪ከዋክብት ፡ ብሩሃን ፡ አባ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወአባ ፡ ጊኦርጊ፤" ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ፤" ፡ አባ ፡ ሚናስ ፤ ሎሙ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ፍቁረ ፡ ወኃደረ ፤ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓ ቶሙ ፡ ወተወሰከ፤‹ ፡ በምግባረ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዝውእቱ!" ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወተምህረ ፡ እምትምህርታቲሆሙ ፡ ወእምአምልኮቶሙ፤!! ፡ ወተጋድሎቶሙ ። ወተለ ዓለ ፤ እምብዙኃን ፡ እምአበው ፡፤ በአምልኮቱ ፣፤ ወተጋድሎቱ ። ወኮኑ፤! ፡ አባ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወአባ ፡ ጊኦርጊ!! ፡ ወካልኣን ፡ አበው ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወቀንአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣን፤! ፡ ወዘበጦ!!፤፤፤። ፡ ዝብጠተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ግዱፈ፣ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ፪አውራኃ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፤፤' ፡ አንሥኦ ፡ 'እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤!ጾ ፡ ጥዑየ፤፤ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይጥሕር ፡ ከመ ፡ አንባሳ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰይጣናት ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ "እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤?" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ። ወመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ላእካኒሁ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወሐዘነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ወበከየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍልጠቱ ፡ እምገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኢትሕዘን ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ እም ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአድነነ ፡ ለት[እዛዘ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ላዕካኒሁ ፡ ለሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ወሜሞ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡
(= Samannoud); he was the only child of his parents. His parents feared God and performed the actions of monks in their fasting, their prayer, their devotion, and their spiritual combat, so that the report of their justice and their virtue was heard among all men and all the fathers. They married their son against his will. When he entered his bridal chamber with her who was called his wife, he agreed with her to keep their bodies in purity and not to defile their virginity. They led a great spiritual combat. After that, the saint Minäs desired to put on the monastic habit and said to her: "It is not fitting that we perform the actions of monks while we remain in the world." Indeed, they wore a hair shirt under their clothes and stood all night watching in prayer and the reading of the books of God. After that, he embraced her, she said goodbye to him, and he went to the monastery of the saint abbä 'Entones (= Anthony). He wanted to get away from his parents, for they were searching for him in every place with a mandate from the emperor to bring him back to his wife. But God was guarding him, and they did not find him at all. The saint Minäs remained in the monastery of Anthony for many days, devoting himself to asceticism and spiritual combat. There was there with him in the monastic state abbä Mikä'el (= Michael), who became patriarch of the city of Alexandria. After that, he descended from the mountain of Anthony and went to the monastery of the saint abbä Magäres (= Macarius) at Asgêtes (= Scetis). This happened in the time of the two luminous stars abbä 'Abrehäm (= Abraham) and abbä Gi'orgi (= George). This father, abbä Minäs, was for them a beloved son; he remained with them in their cave. He redoubled his angelic actions—that is to say, monastic ways of acting—and learned from their teachings and their devotion and spiritual combat. He rose above many fathers by his piety and his spiritual combat. Abbä 'Abrehäm, abbä Gi'orgi, and other fathers admired him. Satan was jealous of him and gave him a blow on the feet; he remained lying on the ground for two months. After that, our Lord Christ raised him up intact; he was roaring like a lion against the demons. After that, our Lord Christ called him to be a bishop. The envoys of the patriarch came to him; he felt great affliction and wept to leave the desert of Scetis. The holy fathers said to him: "Do not be afflicted, for this matter comes from God." He submitted to the commandment of God and went with the envoys of the patriarch. The patriarch consecrated him bishop of the city of
ትመይ፤፤ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸጋ፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሶሙ ፡ ለድውያን ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወይጹሊ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወይፌውሶሙ፤፡ ፡ ሶቤሃ ። ወወሀቦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር፤" ፡ አእ ምሮ ፡ ኅቡኣት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ የአምር ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ሰብእ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ እለ ፡ በሓውርቲሆሙ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ይመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ!፤ ፡ ወያደንኑ ፡ ወይትአዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ፤፤ ፡ ወይትመሀሩ ፡ እምክሩ ። ወኮኑ ፡ 'አሕዛብ ፡ ይመጽኡ!፤"፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ በሓውርት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስምዑ ፡ ትምህርታቲሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ አበ ፡፣ ለ፪ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወአንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ዲቤሆሙ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ሚመቶሙ ። እሉ ፡ እሙ ንቱ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ወቆዝሞስ ፡ ወቴዎድሮስ ፡ ወሚካኤል ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ 'እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍልሶ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፤ ኃላፊ ፡ አእመረ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ፍልሰቱ፤ ፡ ወፈነወ!" ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡'ይኩኑ ፡ ጽኑዓነ፤፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ወይዕቀቡ፤ ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወእምዝ ፤ መጠዎሙ ፡ ለኖላዊሆሙ ፡ ዘበአማን ፡ እግዚእነ፤ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወፈለሰ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ። "ወለሐዉ ፡ ላዕሌሁ፤ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወሐዘኑ ፡ በእንተ ፡ 'ፍልጠቶሙ ፡ እምነ ፡፣ ኖላዊሆሙ!፤ ፡ ቴር ፡ ወአቡሆሙ፤ ፣ የዐቅቦሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ 'ህየንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤?" ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገነዝዎ፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይደልዎ፤፤ ፡ ወረሰይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡
Temay. God gave him the gift of healing the sick. All those who had an illness came to him; he prayed for them to God and healed them immediately. God gave him the knowledge of hidden things, and he knew what was in the minds of people. All the bishops whose territories were close to him came to him, bowed before him, obeyed him, and learned from his advice. The crowds came to him from all regions to hear his teachings. He was the spiritual father of four patriarchs and laid his hand on them at the time of their consecration. These are abbä 'Eleskenderos (= Alexander), Qozmos (= Cosmas), Téwodros (= Theodore), and Mikä'ël (= Michael), patriarchs of the city of Alexandria. When our Lord Christ wanted to make him leave this transitory world, this father knew by the Holy Spirit the day of his departure. He dispatched and had all the people who were in his diocese come, and he prescribed to them to be firm in the right faith and to observe the commandments of the holy Gospel. Then, he handed them over to their true shepherd, our Lord Jesus Christ, left this transitory world, and arrived at Christ whom he loved. All the people groaned over him and were afflicted to be separated from their excellent shepherd, their father who was guarding them on earth in the place of God. Then the people buried him as was fitting for him and placed him in the place he had prescribed for them. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 262] {#po.44.p262}
መካን ፡ 'ዘኮነ ፡ አዘዞሙ፤፥‹ ፡ ቦቱ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ቅዳሴሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ ወክቡር ፡ ወዓቢይ ፡ እምሰማዕታት፤፤ ፣ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ዘየዓቢ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ልዳ ፡ ወዘገብረ ፡ ውስቴታ፤= ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ዓቢያተ ፡ ዘያ ሥተፈሥቃሙ ፡ ለአልባበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ፤ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ 'ዜና ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወየብስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምአ፤ ፡ ዲዮቅልጢያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ዜናሃ፤ ፣፡ 'ለቅድስት ፡ ቤተ፤ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ፤ ፡ ዘይትገበር ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አውሕዮስ፤ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ሰራዊተ ፤ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንህላ፤' ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወበጽሐ ፤ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ በትዕቢት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሕየ ፡ ወአርገ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ጽርህ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ ስእሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ ይሳለቅ ፡ ላእለ፤፤ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወላእለ፤። ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ጊዮርጊስ ። ወኮነ ፡ 'በእዴሁ ፡ በትር፤ ፡ ወዘበጦ ፡ ቦቱ፤፥ ፡ ለሞሕ፤፤ ፡ ዘይበርህ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ሥዕሉ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ፤፤ ፡ ወሰበሮ ። ወወድቀ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡'ስባር፣ ዘሞሕ፤፤ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ኢያእመረ ። ወበጽሐ፤ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ ወረዓድ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወሐመ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወወድቀ፤' ፡
On this day also is the feast of the consecration of the church of the saint, illustrious and great among the martyrs, Giyorgis (= George) the Great in the city of Ledä (= Lydda), and the commemoration of the great miracles and wonders that rejoice the hearts of men, miracles that our Lord Christ did there, his miracles whose report has been heard on sea and on land. When Diyogletiyänos (= Diocletian), the infidel emperor, learned of the fame of the holy church of the saint Giyorgis and the miracles and wonders that were done there, he sent an officer whose name was 'Awhyos (= Eucheus)—and with him many soldiers—to destroy the church. This officer arrived there with arrogance and climbed into the chancel in which was the image of the saint Giyorgis. He began to mock the Christians and the saint Giyorgis. There was a stick in his hand; he struck with it the glass lamp that was shining before the image of the saint Giyorgis and broke it. A piece of glass fell from it and reached the head of this impious man, and he did not


[p. 264] {#po.44.p264}
"ግዱፈ ። ወጾርዎ፤፤ ፡ አብያጺሁ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ይስድዎ፤"።= ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘበጽሐ፤" ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ሰማዕት፤ ። ወእንዘ፤ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ሞተ ፡፤ ውእቱ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ በኃሣር ፡ ዓቢይ፤ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ወገደፍዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ፣07፣ ዘንተ!?" ፡ ዲዮቅልጢያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ፤ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፡ ይሖር፤ ፡ ወያንህላ፤?፤ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለቅዱስ፤?"። ፡ ጊዮርጊስ፤ ። ወኢተአገሠ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እግዚአብሔር፤” ፡ አላ ፡'ፍጡነ ፡ ዘበጦ! ፡ ወአዖረ ፡፣ አዕይንቲሁ ። ወአንሥአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መንግ ሥት!፤ ፣ ወአእተቶ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እመንግሥት፤ ። ወሜሞ! ፡ ለቄስጠንጢ ኖስ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወአርኃወ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ወአእጸወ!” ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወተፈስሑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም፤ ፡ ወኩሎሙ፤"፤ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያ ናት፤፤ ፣ ወፈድፋደሰ፤ ፣፡ ቤተ፤5 ፣ ክርስቲያኑ ፣ ለቅዱስ፤" ፡፣ ክቡር ፡ ኮከበ ፡ ጽባሕ ፤ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት፤-፤ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
realize it. A fear and a great trembling came upon him; he had a headache and fell stretched out on the ground. His companions carried him to take him to their country, for they knew that all that had happened had been because of the saint Giyorgis the martyr. While they were on the way, this impious officer died in great shame; they took him and threw him into the sea. When Diyogletiyänos, the infidel emperor, learned of this, he became very angry. He thought in his heart to go himself to destroy the church of the saint Giyorgis. But God was not patient with him; on the contrary, He struck him quickly, blinded his eyes, and raised the people of the palace against him; God removed him from the empire and established Quastantinos (= Constantine) the Just. He opened the churches and closed the temples of idols. All the ends of the universe rejoiced, as well as all the churches, and particularly the church of the holy and illustrious morning star, the saint Giyorgis the martyr. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዚኖቢስ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ዚቢያ፤" ። እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኮኑ፤፤ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ተባይስ ፡ ወልሂቃን ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወአስተዋደይ ዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ያቅርብዎሙ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ሕርመትክሙ ፡ ወአይቴ ፡ ብሔርክሙ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ምእመናን ፡ በክርስቶስ ፡ ወሀገርነ ፡ ተባይስ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ሠዑ ፡ ለአማልክትየ፤። ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ንሠውዕ ፡ ለአምላክነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ለአጋንንት ። ወተምዕዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ያዕርቅዎሙ ፡ አልባሲሆሙ ፡ ወይስቅልዎሙ ፡ በሥዕርተ ፡ ርእሶሙ ፣ ወይዝብጥዎሙ ፡ በአብትር ። ወጸለዩ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተፈትሐ ፡ ማእሠሪሆሙ ። ወርእይዎሙ ፡፣ ሕዝብ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይለብሱ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ብሩሃን ፡ ወመኩንንኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውር ፡ መንበሮ ፡ ወይተሉ ፡ ድኅሬሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸርሑ ፤ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፀባኦት ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወአንከረ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወይቤ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ዐቢይ፤ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተምዕዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይስቅልዎሙ ፡ በክልኤቱ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ከመ፣ ይትናጸሩ ፤ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ መጽአ ፡ ደመና ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወገልበቦሙ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ መና ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ረከቦሙ ፣ መኩንን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜህሩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወርእዮሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ጸርሑ ፡ በ ፳ቃል ፡እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ነአምን ፡ ከመ፣ ዐቢይ ፡ እምላኮሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ። ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ!፤ ፡ ፪መናብርተ ፡ ወይትክሉ ፡ ውስቴቶን ፡ ፪ሐፃውንተ ፡ ወይደይዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እቶነ ፡
[On this day also were martyrs the saint Zinobis (= Zenobius) and his mother Zibiyä (= Zenobia). These saints were from the city of Tabäyes, and they were notables. They were denounced to the emperor as being Christians. The emperor ordered them to be brought to him and said to them: "What is your religion and where is your country?" They said to him: "We are faithful of Christ and our city is Tabäyes." The emperor said to them: "Sacrifice to my gods." The saints said to him: "We sacrifice to our God Jesus Christ and not to demons." The governor became angry and ordered them to be stripped of their clothes, to be suspended by their hair, and to be beaten with rods. The saints prayed to God, and immediately their bonds were untied. The crowd saw them clothed in garments of light, as well as the governor carrying his seat and following them. When it saw this, the crowd cried out, saying: "Holy, holy, holy is the God of hosts, our God Jesus Christ!" The governor was astonished and said: "Great is the God of the Christians!" Then the governor became angry and ordered them to be suspended on two crosses so that they could see each other. Immediately, a cloud from heaven appeared, enveloped them, and gave them manna. The next day, the governor found them teaching the word of God. Seeing this, the crowd cried out with one voice, saying: "We believe that the God of the Christians is great." The governor ordered again to make two seats, to fix two iron bars on them, and to put them—the saints being seated on them—in a furnace of fire. God saved them, and they were found teaching

እሳት ። ወአድኃኖሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወረከብዎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሜህሩ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ነቢሮሙ ፡ዲበ ፡ውእቱ ፡መንበር ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ መኩንን ፡ መልአ ፡ መዓተ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ይክርዩ ፡ ዓዘቅተ ፡ ዘ፳በእመት፤ ፡ ግድሙ ፡ ወአንደዱ ፡ እሳተ ፣ ወወደይዎሙ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸለዩ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጠፍአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወዓዘቅትኒ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቤት ፡ ርሱይ ። ወጸውዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዓቃቤ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ብለኔ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፀውር ፡ ኮራተ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወርእዮሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ዘንተ፣ ተአምራተ ፡ አእኩትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ 'በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ተኣምር'""" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ መንክር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ቅዱሳኒሁ ። ወሶበ ፣ ኃጥአ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዘከመ ፡፣ ይሬስዮሙ፤ ፣ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎሙ ፡ ወይዕቀቡ ፤ አብድንቲሆሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጌሠም ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውዕይዎሙ ፡ በእሳት ፣ ወይዝርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ነፋስ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀተልዎሙ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ነጐድጓድ ፡ ወፀዓዕ ፡ ወመባርቅት ፡ ወዝናማት ። ወሞቱ ፣ በውእቱ ፡ ድልቅልቅ ፡ ዛወ፪ሰብእ ። ወበሌሊት ፡ ሠረቁ ፡ 'መሃይምናን ፣፤ አብድንቲ ሆሙ ፡ ወቀበርዎሙ ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኀሠሠ ፡ መኩንን‹ዐ ፡ አብድንቲሆሙ ፡ ነገ ርዎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ ወአምነ፤ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ። ጸሎ ቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡
the people sitting on the seats. When the governor saw this, he was filled with anger and ordered a well to be dug whose depth was twenty cubits. They lit a fire in it and threw them into it. As the saints were praying to God, the fire went out, and the well was for them like an installed house. The governor called the keeper of the baths; he was carrying cups of water. Seeing this sign, the crowd praised God for this sign, saying: "Admirable is God through his saints!" As the governor could not find how to treat the saints, he ordered them to be killed and to keep their bodies until the next day to burn them in the fire and scatter their ashes in the air. When they were killed, there was thunder, crashing lightning, flashes, and downpours. In this shaking, fifty-four people died. At night, some faithful stole their bodies and buried them. The next day, when the governor searched for their bodies, they told him all the miracle that had taken place, and the governor believed and became a Christian. May their prayer and their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]
ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ካዕበ""።" ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ መርቆሬዎስ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ እሁ ። ወእሉሰ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ መሲሓውያን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወፈራህያነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወስሙሰ ፡ ለዘየዐቢ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ለብሰ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ሰመ ይዎ ፡ መርቆሬዎስ ። ወለዘይንእስሂ ፡ ስሙ ፡ አቡፈረ፲የ ፡ ወለውእቱሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ለብሰ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፣ ሠመይዎ ፡ ዮሐንስ ። ወእሉሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ልህቁ ፡ ኅቡረ" ፣፡ ሖሩ፣ ኀበ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቴዎድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ሠራዊት ። ወነበሩ ፡ ታሕተ 7ትእዛዘ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ዘይትቀነይ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ይትኤዘዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘይጻምው ፡ በእንተ ፡ ተልእኮ ፡ አኃው ፡ ወመፍቅደ ፡ደብር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትዔገሠ ፡ ሦረ ፡ ክረምት ፡ ወዋዕየ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ያስተጋብኡ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ፍሬያተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወያመጽኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ መካን0 ። ወሎሙሰ ፡ ኢያጥረዩ ፡ ጥሪታተ?" ፡ ውስተ ፣ ዓለም ። ወዓዲ ፡ ርኅቁ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ጣዕማተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወመብልፅዕ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ወነዊም ፡ ወይጸውሙ ፡ በበ ፪ወበበ፻ ዕለት ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተምህሩ ፡ ጽሒፈ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ በቅብጢ ፡ ወበዓረቢ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ መምህር ፡ ወፈጸሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ፅለት ፡ ከመዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወአሜሃ ፡ ጸንዑ፤‹ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ በጐጐዓ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ። አሜሃ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ወአስተዋደይዎሙ ፡'ኅበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገር0ጾ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ አግብኦሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ብህንሳ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ተስእሎሙ ፥፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አማንኑ ፡ አንትሙሰ ፡ መጣዓውያን" ። ወቅዱሳንሰ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕ
[On this day also is the death of the saint Margorëwos (= Mercurius) the New and of Yohannes (= John) his brother. They were the children of Christian people and feared God. The name of the elder of them was Sanay; when he put on the monastic habit, he was called Margorëwos; as for the younger, his name was 'Abufaraj, and he, when he put on the monastic habit, was called Yohannes. When they had grown up together, they went to the monastery of the saint Téwodros (= Theodore) the general. They remained under the authority of a just old man who served God; they obeyed him in every tiring action for the service of the brothers and the needs of the monastery, enduring the cold of winter and the heat of the sun. They gathered many products of the earth and brought them to this place (= the monastery). But for themselves, they did not acquire goods in the world. Moreover, they were far from all the sweetnesses of the world—food, drink, and sleep—and they both fasted every two and three days. They also learned to write books in Coptic and Arabic without having a teacher, and they accomplished all the monastic law. While they were thus, one day, an angel of God came and told them that they would arrive at the combat of martyrdom. They were then firm in the Holy Spirit and rose in haste, rejoicing; then they arrived in their city. Then the people of their city gathered and denounced them to the governor of the city. He sent them to the governor of Behnesä (= Behnesah). He questioned them and said to them: "Is it true that you are idolaters?" The saints said to him:

"We are Christians openly, and we have no other God than our Lord Jesus Christ." When the governor heard this, he was filled with anger and ordered iron chains to be put on their necks and to have them paraded throughout the city. Then, they were incarcerated in prison. While they were there, the angel of God visited them many times. It happened, after five months, that the governor had them brought out, had them stand before him, and said to them: "Abandon your faith." They refused him. The governor wanted to frighten them by saying: "I am going to burn your bodies in the fire." But they were not afraid of his words at all. When he saw the constancy of their spirit, he sent them back to prison. After many days, another governor came and wanted to save them and release them. Immediately, the people of the city took him to task and said to him: "If you do not kill them, we will denounce you to the emperor." He ordered a fire to be lit quickly to frighten the saints, but they dared to enter it. He wanted to spare them again and threw them back into prison. He ordered again to have them brought out and to frighten them with sharpened swords. But the heart of the saints did not weaken, and one could not make them change their words. Then, the governor ordered, for fear of the crowd, to cut off their heads. They cut off the head of Yohannes first; the head of Yohannes jumped, and a great cry came out of it like a cry of joy, so that all the people who heard it were astonished. Then they cut off the head of the saint Margorëwos, and they finished their holy martyrdom. They threw their bodies into the fire, and the fire went out immediately and did not touch their bodies or their clothes at all. When the governor saw this, he ordered their bodies to be guarded so that the Christians would not steal them. The next day, when they looked, they found a spring of water under the pit where they had thrown them. After that, many miracles appeared from it. May their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]

[READING] FOR THE 8TH OF HEDAR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day is the commemoration of the feast of the four animals that have no body; they are the chariot of God, carriers of his seat, as John the evangelist is witness concerning them in his vision 'Abu qalamsis (= Apocalypse) by saying: I saw a throne in heaven, and he who was sitting was like a lamp of gold. ንተ፤! ። 8አምሳለ ፡ ገጸ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወዳግማይ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ላህም ፡ ወሳልሣይ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወራብዕ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ንስር ። ወለለ88እምኔሆሙ ፡ ጸ፪ክ ንፍ፤፤ ። ወእሙንቱ ፡ ይጸርሑ ፡ 'ሌሊተ ፤ ወመዓልተ፤" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ምሉዕ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወምድር ፡ ቅድ ሳተ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ልዑል ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ቤት ፡ ምሉዕ ፡ እምስብሐቲሁ ፡ "ወኪሩቤል ፡ ወሱራፌል ፡ የአውድዎ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ልዑል፤ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ መፍርህ ። ወለለ፳8እምኔሆሙ ። ክንፍ ። በ፪' ፡ ይከድኑ ፡ ገጾሙ ፣ በ፪ክንፍ ፡ይሰርሩ! ፡ በ፪ይከድኑ ፡ እግሮሙ ። ወይጸርሁ!፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ምሉእ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ወምድር ፡ ቅድ ሳተ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ወይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ተጽእነ ፡ ላእለ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወሰረረ ። ወካ ፅበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ ወአድለቅለቃ ፡ ለምድር ። ወይቤ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፡ ነቢይ" ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሰረገላ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ይነፍኅ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወውስቴቱ ፡ ደማና ፡ እንዘ" ፡ የአውዳ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወብርሃን ፡ ወመብረ[ቅ ። ወይጸውርዋ ፡ ፪እንስሳ ፤ ወለለ ፳8እምኔሆሙ ፡ 8ገጽ ፤ ወፄሄክንፍ ። ወእሙ[ን]ቱ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወእምኔሆሙ ፡ ፳ አምሳለ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወኢያጸርዑ ፡ እምስብሐቲሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፤ ኢሌሊተ ፡ ወኢመአልተ ፡ ወይጸርሁ ፡ 'እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ምሉእ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ወምድር ፡ ቅድሳተ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ወይቤ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሃሌሉያ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ወክብር። ፡ ወኀይል ፡ ወበረከት ፡ ለአምላክነ ። ወሰገዱ ፡ 8እንስሳ ፡ ወ፳ወ፪ካህናት፤ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር፥" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሃሌ ሉያ ፡ ይነግሥ?" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አሓዜ ፡ ኩሉ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ "እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ስብሕዎ ፡ ለአምላክነ ፣ በመሰንቆ ፣ ወበመዝሙር ፡ ሃሌ ፡ ሉያ ፡ ወሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ 'ብዙኅ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነጐድጓድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ነግሠ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አኃዜ ፤ ኩሉ ። ወብዙሃት ፡ ፅመጻሕፍት ፡ ብሊት ፡ ወኀዳስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በእንተ፤ ፣ ክብሮሙ ፡ ላዕሉ ፡ ፪እንስሳ ። ወእግዚአብሔር ፣ ረሰዮሙ፡ ፡ ቅሩባነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስአልዎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ። ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይስእል ፡ "በእንተ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመህያው ፡ ወገጸ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ይስእል ፡ በእንተ ፡ አራዊት ፡ ወገጸ ፡ ላህም ፡ ይስእል ፡ በእንተ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ ወገጸ ፡ ንስር ፡ ይስእል ፡ በእንተ ፡ አእዋፍ ። እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ቅሩባን ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ ኃይላተ ፡ ሰማያዊያን ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ እስመ፥ ፡ መምህራነ ፡ ቤተ ፡
throne) four animals filled with eyes: one was like a face of a lion; the second was like a face of a bull; the third was like a face of a man; the fourth was like a face of an eagle. Each of them had six wings, and they cried day and night, saying: "Holy, holy, holy is the perfect God of hosts! The heavens and the earth are full of the holiness of your glory!" 'Isayeyäs (= Isaiah) the prophet also says: I saw the Lord of hosts sitting on his high throne. The whole house was filled with his glory. The cherubim and the seraphim surrounded him; he was very high and feared. Each of them had six wings. With two of them they veiled their face; with two other wings they flew; with the last two they veiled their feet. They cried, saying: "Holy, holy, holy is the perfect God of hosts! The heavens and the earth are full of the holiness of your glory!" Däwit (= David) the prophet says: He mounted on a cherub and flew. He also says: He who is sitting on the cherubim makes the earth tremble. Hezqe'ël (= Ezekiel) the prophet says concerning this chariot: I saw: a wind was blowing from heaven; there was a cloud surrounded by fire, light, and lightning, and four animals were carrying it. Each of them had four faces and six wings. They were going before them. One of them was like a face of a man. They did not interrupt the praise of God neither night nor day and cried, saying: "Holy, holy, holy is the perfect God of hosts! The heavens and the earth are full of the holiness of your glory." John the evangelist says: I heard a voice that said: "Alleluia! Salvation, glory, power, and blessing are to our God." And the four animals and the twenty-four elders prostrated themselves before God, saying: "Alleluia! He reigns, the God master of all." After that, I heard a voice saying: "Praise our God with a harp and psalms, alleluia!" I heard a voice of many crowds, like thunder, saying: "He has entered into his reign, the God master of all." Many books of the Old and New Testaments are witnesses of the glory of these four animals. God has put them close to him so that they might pray for all creatures. The one with the face of a man prays for the creatures of the human race; the one with the face of a lion prays for wild animals; the one with the face of a bull prays for domestic animals; the one with the face of an eagle prays for birds. Indeed, they are much closer to God than all the heavenly powers. Because of that, the doctors of
ክርስቲያን ፡ አጽንዑ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ወኃነጹ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሹሉ ፡ መካናት፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ እለት ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይስእልዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ። ትንብልናሆሙ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
the Church have confirmed their commemoration, and churches have been built for them in every place today, for they pray to God for the race of man. May their intercession before God be with us, amen.


[p. 272] {#po.44.p272}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለአፍኒን ፣ ሊቀ ፣ መላእክት ፣፤ 8እም፪ሊቃነ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ዘየዐቅብ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሱራፌል ፡ ዘበእንቲአሁ ፡ "ሄኖክ ፡ ይቤ፥ ፡ ወ[የ]ዓውድዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ቤት ፤ ዓውዶ ፡ ሱራፌል ፡ ወኪሩቤል ፡ ወአፍኒንየ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይነውሙ ፡ ወየዓቅቡ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወባቲ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also is the feast of 'Afnin (= Ophanim) the archangel, one of the nine archangels, who is the one who guards the throne of glory with the seraphim, the one of whom Enoch says: They surrounded this house; around it were the seraphim, the cherubim, and 'Afnin; they are those who do not sleep and guard the throne of his (= God's) glory. May his intercession be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever.] [On this day also is the commemoration of Yohannes (= John) the archpriest, and in him, the Cross appeared to Quastantinos (= Constantine). May their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ፤ ፡ ቅፍርያ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አረማዌ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መኩንኖሙ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ እመዋዕል ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘምኔተ ፡ አባ ፡ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ መርቆሬዎስ ። ወተነበየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ ። ወበኅዳጥ ፤ መዋዕል ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ሠራዊቶ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፄወው ፡ ወያጥፍእ ፡ አድባራተ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ምኔተ ፡ አባ ፣ ጳኩሚስ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትፈቅድ ። ወይቤ ፡ እፈቅድ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ እርአዮ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ቅፍሪ ፡ አግኃሦ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያልብሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ አበ፣ምኔት፣ይቤሎ ፡ኦሆ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ፡ ተርፈ ፤ ባሕቲቱ ። ወአልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወተጸምደ ፡ ለጾም ፡ ወለጸሎት ። ወቀንዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወአንሥአ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ መስተዋድየ ፡ ወአስተናስአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ መኳንንተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይፈቅዱ፥ ፡ ይዕግትዎ ፡ ለደብር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዝየ ፣ ወልደ ፡ ነጋሚ ፡ ዘአማሰነ ፡ ሀገረነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኃጥእዎ ፡ በረድኤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ "ቀተልዎ ፡ ለመስተዋድይ" ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ በበሱባዔ ፡ ወኢይጥዕም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ተርሙስ፤ ። ወበውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አማሰነ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ውእተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይቀትል ፡ እንስሳሆሙ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ አባ ፡ ቅፍሪ ፡ ወአኃዘ ፡ እዝኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ወአሠሮ ፡ በሀብል ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ፲ወ፪ዓመተ ፤ ወሞተ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ቅፍሪ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመርሖ ፡ መልአከ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ወበጽሐ ፡፤ በአሐቲ፥ ፡ ሌሊት ። ወርእየ ፡ በህየ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ፣፥፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also died the saint abbä Qefreyä (= Copronius). This saint was a pagan, son of their (= the people of his city?) governor. One day, a monk arrived in his city who was from the monastery of abbä Päkuemis (= Pachomius) whose name was Margorëwos (= Mercurius). He prophesied that Qefreyä would become a Christian. A few days later, Qefreyä gathered his soldiers and went to the land of Egypt to take prisoners and destroy monasteries. When he arrived at the monastery of abbä Päkuemis, the abbot went out to him and said to him: "What do you want?" Qefreyä said: "I want to see the abbot." The abbot said to him: "It is I." When Qefri heard this, he took him aside, alone, and asked him to clothe him in the monastic habit. Hearing this, the abbot said to him: "Yes." Then his soldiers returned to their country; he remained alone. The abbot clothed him in the monastic habit, and Qefri devoted himself to fasting and prayer. Satan was jealous of him and stirred up an accuser against him; he raised the governors against him so that they wanted to besiege the monastery, saying: "There is here the son of a king who destroyed our city." As, with the help of God, they did not find him, they killed the accuser. This saint remained fasting every week and tasted only lupin. In those days, a wild animal of the desert devastated the monastery by killing its cattle. Abbä Qefri went to it, grabbed its ear like a sheep, and tied it with a rope; it remained there twelve years and died. The saint abbä Qefri went to Jerusalem, an angel of God guiding him, and arrived there in one night. He saw great holy mysteries there, returned to his dwelling, and died in peace. May his intercession be with our king 'Iyäsu and with his dear Za-Manfas Qeddus for ever and ever.]


[p. 274] {#po.44.p274}
ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡'ስምዓ ፡ ኮኑ" ፡ 8፻ጅ ወ፪ ነፍስ ፡ ማኅበራነ ፡ ኢስድሮስ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ንጉሥነ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also became martyrs four hundred and eighty-four souls, companions of 'Isderos (= Isidore). May their blessing be with our king 'Iyäsu for ever and ever.]

[READING] FOR THE 9TH OF HEDAR In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the saint abbä Yeshaq (= Isaac), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarchs, he is the forty-first. This father was from the people of the city of Berules (= Bourlos), and his parents were very rich and feared God. After long days, they begot this holy father and rejoiced over him with great joy. When they brought him to the bishop so that he might baptize him with the Christian baptism, the bishop saw on his head a cross of light when he baptized him. The bishop took the hand of the little child, placed it on his head, and prophesied about him, saying that he would be established at the head of the Church of God. The bishop said to his father: "Watch over this child, for he will be a chosen tool for God." When he had grown a little, they taught him writing and spiritual science. He read the history of the saints. He abandoned his parents, went to the monastery of abbä Magäres (= Macarius), and became a monk with the father abbä Zakäryäs (= Zacharias), the abbot. An angel of God had informed the abbot of the arrival of this saint, and the abbot welcomed him and rejoiced over it. One day, a just man saw him in the church and prophesied about him, saying: "This Yeshaq will be established at the head of the Church of Christ." In those days, the patriarch abbä Yohannes (= John) looked for an instructed man to help him and be his secretary. This illustrious father abbä Yeshaq was recommended to him. He dispatched, had him come, and gave him a letter to write. Yeshaq spoiled it knowingly so that the patriarch would let him go to the desert of Asqêtes (= Scetis), for he hated the vain glory of men. When the patriarch abbä Yohannes understood that he had spoiled it knowingly so that he would let him go, the saint abbä Yohannes said to him: "You have written well, and I will not let you go away from here." When abbä Yeshaq understood that he would not let him go, he showed all his knowledge, his wisdom, and the beautiful writing that he knew. The patriarch rejoiced over it much. A few days later, he returned to the desert of Scetis. When the death of abbä Yohannes the patriarch was near, he asked our Lord Christ to indicate to him who was the one who would be instituted in his place as patriarch after him. Our Lord said to him in a vision: "Your disciple Yeshaq will be worthy of this charge after you." The saint abbä Yohannes made recommendations to the

people and informed them that it was his disciple Yeshaq who would be instituted after him. When abbä Yohannes died, they took this saint abbä Yeshaq and instituted him patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He illuminated the Church during his life and restored during his life many churches, among them the church of the saint Märgos (= Mark) the evangelist apostle and the house of the patriarchate. A great misfortune and many trials happened to this father. He remained on the seat of Märgos the evangelist three years and a half. It is written in the life of this holy father that he prescribed that a patriarch should only be consecrated on a Sunday. May the prayer and the blessing of this father be with us, amen.

On this day also gathered the three hundred and eighteen father bishops in the city of Niqyä (= Nicaea) in the time of Quastantinos (= Constantine), the just emperor. There were among them the four patriarchs: abbä 'Eskenderos (= Alexander), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; Yonäkendinos (= Innocent), patriarch of the city of Rome; the patriarch of the city of Constantinople; and the patriarch of the city of Antioch. Their assembly took place concerning 'Aryos (= Arius), who was a priest of the people of the city of Alexandria, who was wandering and saying that the Son was created in every way. These saints were illustrious fathers, athletes of God. There were among them some who were like the apostles: they had raised the dead, healed the sick, and done great miracles and wonders. There were among them some whom they had tortured because of the right faith. There were among them some to whom they had torn out the eyes. There were some to whom they had cut off the hands and feet. There were some to whom they had broken the teeth, torn out the nails, and broken the ribs. There was among them a bishop of the city of Mar'äs (= Marach), his name was Tomäs (= Thomas). The impious Manichaeans had imprisoned him for twenty-one years and had tortured him without pity. Every year they had cut off one of his limbs. His hands and feet were cut off, his teeth and molars broken, his nostrils and lips cut off. His body was black from the burning of the fire. Many people believed that he was dead and performed commemorations for him as for the martyrs. When these father bishops arrived at the city of Nicaea, the emperor Quastantinos prepared for them a vast and spacious hall and had seats placed there so that the father bishops might sit there. He made his own seat smaller than their seats and began with Tomäs, the bishop of the city of Mar'as: he prostrated himself before him and kissed the place of all his limbs that had been cut off from his body. Then he gave them (= the bishops) his royal scepter, his sword, the ring from his finger, and said to them: "Behold, I give you
